argobot — Third-Party Software Notices
======================================

The argobot container image includes the third-party open-source Go modules
listed below, statically linked into the argobot binary. Each is distributed
under its own license, the full text of which is reproduced further down in
this file. Operating-system packages from the base image are covered
separately in OS_LICENSE_NOTICE.txt. This file is generated by
scripts/gen-third-party-licenses.sh; do not edit it by hand.
